inline constexpr int
    kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion = 1;
inline constexpr int
    kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers = 1000;

// These immutable value types describe a read-only, point-in-time,
// non-persistent, and non-authoritative evidence assessment. They intentionally
// contain no campaign success decision or follow-up authorization.
struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity
{
    const long long campaignApprovalId;
    const std::string identityCanonical;
    const std::string identityHash;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity(
        long long campaignApprovalId,
        std::string identityCanonical,
        std::string identityHash);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&) const =
        default;
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity
{
    const long long materializationId;
    const long long campaignApprovalId;
    const int contractVersion;
    const int memberCount;
    const std::string identityCanonical;
    const std::string identityHash;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity(
        long long materializationId,
        long long campaignApprovalId,
        int contractVersion,
        int memberCount,
        std::string identityCanonical,
        std::string identityHash);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&)
        const = default;
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity
{
    const int memberOrdinal;
    const long long materializationMemberId;
    const long long rankingMemberId;
    const long long recommendationId;
    const long long sourceExperimentId;
    const long long proposalId;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity(
        int memberOrdinal,
        long long materializationMemberId,
        long long rankingMemberId,
        long long recommendationId,
        long long sourceExperimentId,
        long long proposalId);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity&) const =
        default;
};

    NumericDelta,
    Unsupported

            comparisonSupport);

        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric&) const = default;

    Comparable,
    ContextChanged,
    MissingSourceEvidence,
    MissingResultEvidence,

    MetricUnavailable,
    UnsupportedComparison
};

    WindowSize,
    LabelDefinition,
    MetricIdentity,
    InferenceRange

        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification

            contextDifferences);

        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison&) const =
        default;
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment
{
    static constexpr bool readOnly = true;
    static constexpr bool pointInTime = true;
    static constexpr bool persistent = false;
    static constexpr bool authoritative = false;
    static constexpr bool declaresCampaignSuccess = false;

#include "ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp"

#include "ExperimentRecommendation.hpp"
#include "ExperimentRecommendationCampaignMaterialization.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <locale>
#include <set>
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <tuple>
#include <utility>

namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation
{
namespace
{

void RequireText(const std::string& value, const char* error)
{
    if (value.empty() || value.find('\0') != std::string::npos)
        throw std::invalid_argument(error);
}

void RequireOptionalPositive(
    const std::optional<long long>& value,
    const char* error)
{
    if (value && *value <= 0) throw std::invalid_argument(error);
}

std::string LengthPrefixed(const std::string& value)
{
    return std::to_string(value.size()) + ":" + value;
}

std::string OptionalLongLong(const std::optional<long long>& value)
{
    return value ? std::to_string(*value) : "null";
}

std::string OptionalDouble(const std::optional<double>& value)
{
    return value ? CanonicalRecommendationDouble(*value) : "null";
}

void AppendContext(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext& context)
{
    out << LengthPrefixed(context.symbol) << ',' << context.predictionHorizon
        << ',' << CanonicalRecommendationDouble(context.threshold) << ','
        << context.windowSize << ',' << LengthPrefixed(context.labelDefinition)
        << ',' << LengthPrefixed(context.inferenceRangeStart) << ','
        << LengthPrefixed(context.inferenceRangeEnd);
}

void AppendMetric(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric& metric)
{
    out << LengthPrefixed(metric.name) << ',' << OptionalDouble(metric.value)
        << ','
        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupportText(
               metric.comparisonSupport);
}

void AppendSourceEvidence(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>&
        evidence)
{
    if (!evidence)
    {
        out << "absent";
        return;
    }
    out << "present," << evidence->sourceExperimentId << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->sourceModelId) << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->sourceAnalysisId) << ',';
    AppendContext(out, evidence->context);
    out << ',';
    AppendMetric(out, evidence->metric);
}

void AppendResultEvidence(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>&
        evidence)
{
    if (!evidence)
    {
        out << "absent";
        return;
    }
    out << "present," << evidence->experimentId << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->modelId) << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->inferenceResultId) << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->analysisResultId) << ','
        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultStateText(
               evidence->state)
        << ',';
    AppendContext(out, evidence->context);
    out << ',';
    AppendMetric(out, evidence->metric);
}

std::string AssessmentCanonicalText(
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity& campaign,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
        materialization,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary& summary,
    const std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember>& members)
{
    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << "experiment_recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_v1"
        << ";assessment_contract_version="
        << kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion
        << ";campaign_approval_id=" << campaign.campaignApprovalId
        << ";campaign_identity_hash="
        << LengthPrefixed(campaign.identityHash)
        << ";materialization_id=" << materialization.materializationId
        << ";materialization_contract_version="
        << materialization.contractVersion
        << ";materialization_identity_hash="
        << LengthPrefixed(materialization.identityHash)
        << ";member_count=" << summary.memberCount;
    for (const auto& member : members)
    {
        out << ";member=" << member.identity.memberOrdinal << ','
            << member.identity.materializationMemberId << ','
            << member.identity.rankingMemberId << ','
            << member.identity.recommendationId << ','
            << member.identity.sourceExperimentId << ','
            << member.identity.proposalId << ",source=";
        AppendSourceEvidence(out, member.sourceEvidence);
        out << ",result=";
        AppendResultEvidence(out, member.resultEvidence);
        out << ",classification="
            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
                   member.comparison.classification)
            << ",delta=" << OptionalDouble(member.comparison.delta)
            << ",context_difference_count="
            << member.comparison.contextDifferences.size();
        for (const auto difference : member.comparison.contextDifferences)
            out << ','
                << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifferenceText(
                       difference);
    }
    out << ";comparable_count=" << summary.comparableCount
        << ";context_changed_count=" << summary.contextChangedCount
        << ";missing_source_evidence_count="
        << summary.missingSourceEvidenceCount
        << ";missing_result_evidence_count="
        << summary.missingResultEvidenceCount
        << ";result_not_terminal_count=" << summary.resultNotTerminalCount
        << ";metric_unavailable_count=" << summary.metricUnavailableCount
        << ";unsupported_comparison_count="
        << summary.unsupportedComparisonCount
        << ";positive_delta_count=" << summary.positiveDeltaCount
        << ";zero_delta_count=" << summary.zeroDeltaCount
        << ";negative_delta_count=" << summary.negativeDeltaCount;
    return out.str();
}

struct MutableSummary
{
    int comparableCount = 0;
    int contextChangedCount = 0;
    int missingSourceEvidenceCount = 0;
    int missingResultEvidenceCount = 0;
    int resultNotTerminalCount = 0;
    int metricUnavailableCount = 0;
    int unsupportedComparisonCount = 0;
    int positiveDeltaCount = 0;
    int zeroDeltaCount = 0;
    int negativeDeltaCount = 0;
};

void CountComparison(
    MutableSummary& summary,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison& comparison)
{
    switch (comparison.classification)
    {
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            Comparable:
            ++summary.comparableCount;
            if (*comparison.delta > 0.0)
                ++summary.positiveDeltaCount;
            else if (*comparison.delta < 0.0)
                ++summary.negativeDeltaCount;
            else
                ++summary.zeroDeltaCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            ContextChanged:
            ++summary.contextChangedCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            MissingSourceEvidence:
            ++summary.missingSourceEvidenceCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            MissingResultEvidence:
            ++summary.missingResultEvidenceCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            ResultNotTerminal:
            ++summary.resultNotTerminalCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            MetricUnavailable:
            ++summary.metricUnavailableCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            UnsupportedComparison:
            ++summary.unsupportedComparisonCount;
            break;
    }
}

} // namespace

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity(
        long long campaignApprovalIdValue,
        std::string identityCanonicalValue,
        std::string identityHashValue)
    : campaignApprovalId(campaignApprovalIdValue),
      identityCanonical(std::move(identityCanonicalValue)),
      identityHash(std::move(identityHashValue))
{
    if (campaignApprovalId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_id_invalid");
    RequireText(identityCanonical,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_missing");
    if (identityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(identityCanonical))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity(
        long long materializationIdValue,
        long long campaignApprovalIdValue,
        int contractVersionValue,
        int memberCountValue,
        std::string identityCanonicalValue,
        std::string identityHashValue)
    : materializationId(materializationIdValue),
      campaignApprovalId(campaignApprovalIdValue),
      contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
      memberCount(memberCountValue),
      identityCanonical(std::move(identityCanonicalValue)),
      identityHash(std::move(identityHashValue))
{
    if (materializationId <= 0 || campaignApprovalId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_id_invalid");
    if (memberCount <= 0 ||
        memberCount > kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_member_count_invalid");
    RequireText(identityCanonical,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_identity_missing");
    if (identityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(identityCanonical))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_identity_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity(
        int memberOrdinalValue,
        long long materializationMemberIdValue,
        long long rankingMemberIdValue,
        long long recommendationIdValue,
        long long sourceExperimentIdValue,
        long long proposalIdValue)
    : memberOrdinal(memberOrdinalValue),
      materializationMemberId(materializationMemberIdValue),
      rankingMemberId(rankingMemberIdValue),
      recommendationId(recommendationIdValue),
      sourceExperimentId(sourceExperimentIdValue),
      proposalId(proposalIdValue)
{
    if (memberOrdinal <= 0 || materializationMemberId <= 0 ||
        rankingMemberId <= 0 || recommendationId <= 0 ||
        sourceExperimentId <= 0 || proposalId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_identity_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric(
        std::string nameValue,
        std::optional<double> valueValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport
            comparisonSupportValue)
    : name(std::move(nameValue)),
      value(valueValue),
      comparisonSupport(comparisonSupportValue)
{
    RequireText(name,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_metric_name_invalid");
    if (value && !std::isfinite(*value))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_metric_value_nonfinite");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext(
        std::string symbolValue,
        int predictionHorizonValue,
        double thresholdValue,
        int windowSizeValue,
        std::string labelDefinitionValue,
        std::string inferenceRangeStartValue,
        std::string inferenceRangeEndValue)
    : symbol(std::move(symbolValue)),
      predictionHorizon(predictionHorizonValue),
      threshold(thresholdValue == 0.0 ? 0.0 : thresholdValue),
      windowSize(windowSizeValue),
      labelDefinition(std::move(labelDefinitionValue)),
      inferenceRangeStart(std::move(inferenceRangeStartValue)),
      inferenceRangeEnd(std::move(inferenceRangeEndValue))
{
    RequireText(symbol,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_symbol_invalid");
    RequireText(labelDefinition,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_label_definition_invalid");
    RequireText(inferenceRangeStart,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_inference_range_invalid");
    RequireText(inferenceRangeEnd,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_inference_range_invalid");
    if (predictionHorizon <= 0 || windowSize <= 0 || !std::isfinite(threshold))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence(
        long long sourceExperimentIdValue,
        std::optional<long long> sourceModelIdValue,
        std::optional<long long> sourceAnalysisIdValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext contextValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric metricValue)
    : sourceExperimentId(sourceExperimentIdValue),
      sourceModelId(sourceModelIdValue),
      sourceAnalysisId(sourceAnalysisIdValue),
      context(std::move(contextValue)),
      metric(std::move(metricValue))
{
    if (sourceExperimentId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_source_experiment_id_invalid");
    RequireOptionalPositive(sourceModelId,
#include "../Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp"

#include "../Sources/ExperimentRecommendation.hpp"
#include "../Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignMaterialization.hpp"

#include <cassert>
#include <cmath>
#include <limits>
#include <optional>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

using namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation;

namespace
{

using Classification =
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification;
using Context = RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext;
using ContextDifference =
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference;
using MemberEvidence = RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence;
using Metric = RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric;
using MetricSupport =
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport;
using ResultEvidence = RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence;
using ResultState = RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState;
using SourceEvidence = RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence;

Context ComparableContext(
    const std::string& symbol = "eurusd",
    int horizon = 12,
    double threshold = 0.0008,
    int windowSize = 96,
    const std::string& labelDefinition = "three_class_forward_return_v1",
    const std::string& inferenceRangeStart = "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
    const std::string& inferenceRangeEnd = "2026-03-31T23:59:59Z")
{
    return {symbol, horizon, threshold, windowSize, labelDefinition,
        inferenceRangeStart, inferenceRangeEnd};
}

Metric NumericMetric(
    std::optional<double> value,
    const std::string& name = "infer_accuracy")
{
    return {name, value, MetricSupport::NumericDelta};
}

SourceEvidence Source(
    long long sourceExperimentId = 501,
    Context context = ComparableContext(),
    Metric metric = NumericMetric(0.70))
{
    return {sourceExperimentId, sourceExperimentId + 100,
        sourceExperimentId + 200, std::move(context), std::move(metric)};
}

ResultEvidence Result(
    long long experimentId = 901,
    ResultState state = ResultState::Terminal,
    Context context = ComparableContext(),
    Metric metric = NumericMetric(0.75))
{
    return {experimentId, experimentId + 100, experimentId + 200,
        experimentId + 300, state, std::move(context), std::move(metric)};
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity MemberIdentity(
    int ordinal)
{
    return {ordinal, 100 + ordinal, 200 + ordinal, 300 + ordinal,
        500 + ordinal, 600 + ordinal};
}

MemberEvidence Member(
    int ordinal,
    std::optional<SourceEvidence> source,
    std::optional<ResultEvidence> result)
{
    return {MemberIdentity(ordinal), std::move(source), std::move(result)};
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity CampaignIdentity()
{
    const std::string canonical = "campaign-approval-identity";
    return {41, canonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonical)};
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity
MaterializationIdentity(int memberCount)
{
    const std::string canonical = "campaign-materialization-identity";
    return {42, 41, kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationContractVersion,
        memberCount, canonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonical)};
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison Compare(
    std::optional<SourceEvidence> source,
    std::optional<ResultEvidence> result)
{
    return CompareRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
        source, result);
}

void AssertContextChanged(
    const SourceEvidence& source,
    const ResultEvidence& result,
    std::vector<ContextDifference> expected)
{
    const auto comparison = Compare(source, result);
    assert(comparison.classification == Classification::ContextChanged);
    assert(!comparison.delta);
    assert(comparison.contextDifferences == expected);
}

template <typename Function>
void AssertInvalidArgument(Function&& function)
{
    try
    {
        std::forward<Function>(function)();
        assert(false);
    }
    catch (const std::invalid_argument&)
    {
    }
}

} // namespace

int main()
{
    static_assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::readOnly);
    static_assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::pointInTime);
    static_assert(!RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::persistent);
    static_assert(!RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::authoritative);
    static_assert(
        !RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::declaresCampaignSuccess);
    static_assert(!std::is_assignable_v<
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity>);
    static_assert(!std::is_assignable_v<
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext&,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext>);
    static_assert(!std::is_assignable_v<
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember&,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember>);
    static_assert(!std::is_assignable_v<
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment&,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment>);

    const auto source = Source();
    const auto result = Result();
    const auto identical = Compare(source, result);
    assert(identical.classification == Classification::Comparable);
    assert(identical.delta);
    assert(std::abs(*identical.delta - (0.75 - 0.70)) < 1e-15);
    assert(identical.contextDifferences.empty());
    assert(identical == Compare(source, result));

    const auto zero = Compare(Source(),
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(0.70)));
    assert(zero.classification == Classification::Comparable);
    assert(zero.delta && *zero.delta == 0.0 && !std::signbit(*zero.delta));
    const auto negative = Compare(Source(),
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(0.60)));
    assert(negative.classification == Classification::Comparable);
    assert(negative.delta && *negative.delta < 0.0);

    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal,
            ComparableContext("gbpusd"), NumericMetric(0.75)),
        {ContextDifference::Symbol});
    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal,
            ComparableContext("eurusd", 24), NumericMetric(0.75)),
        {ContextDifference::PredictionHorizon});
    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal,
            ComparableContext("eurusd", 12, 0.0010), NumericMetric(0.75)),
        {ContextDifference::Threshold});
    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal,
            ComparableContext("eurusd", 12, 0.0008, 192),
            NumericMetric(0.75)),
        {ContextDifference::WindowSize});
    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal,
            ComparableContext("eurusd", 12, 0.0008, 96,
                "binary_forward_return_v2"),
            NumericMetric(0.75)),
        {ContextDifference::LabelDefinition});
    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal,
            ComparableContext("eurusd", 12, 0.0008, 96,
                "three_class_forward_return_v1", "2026-02-01T00:00:00Z",
                "2026-03-31T23:59:59Z"),
            NumericMetric(0.75)),
        {ContextDifference::InferenceRange});

    // Equal floating-point payloads do not make differently named metrics
    // comparable.
    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(0.70, "leader_score")),
        {ContextDifference::MetricIdentity});
    AssertContextChanged(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(0.70, "validation_accuracy")),
        {ContextDifference::MetricIdentity});

    const auto allContextChanged = Compare(Source(),
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal,
            ComparableContext("gbpusd", 24, 0.0010, 192,
                "binary_forward_return_v2", "2026-02-01T00:00:00Z",
                "2026-04-30T23:59:59Z"),
            NumericMetric(0.75, "leader_score")));
    assert(allContextChanged.classification == Classification::ContextChanged);
    assert(!allContextChanged.delta);
    assert(allContextChanged.contextDifferences ==
        std::vector<ContextDifference>({ContextDifference::Symbol,
            ContextDifference::PredictionHorizon, ContextDifference::Threshold,
            ContextDifference::WindowSize, ContextDifference::LabelDefinition,
            ContextDifference::MetricIdentity,
            ContextDifference::InferenceRange}));

    const auto missingSource = Compare(std::nullopt, result);
    assert(missingSource.classification == Classification::MissingSourceEvidence);
    assert(!missingSource.delta && missingSource.contextDifferences.empty());
    const auto missingResult = Compare(source, std::nullopt);
    assert(missingResult.classification == Classification::MissingResultEvidence);
    assert(!missingResult.delta && missingResult.contextDifferences.empty());
    assert(Compare(std::nullopt, std::nullopt).classification ==
        Classification::MissingSourceEvidence);

    const auto notTerminal = Compare(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::NotTerminal,
            ComparableContext("different"), NumericMetric(0.99)));
    assert(notTerminal.classification == Classification::ResultNotTerminal);
    assert(!notTerminal.delta && notTerminal.contextDifferences.empty());

    const auto missingSourceMetric = Compare(
        Source(501, ComparableContext(), NumericMetric(std::nullopt)), result);
    assert(missingSourceMetric.classification ==
        Classification::MetricUnavailable);
    assert(!missingSourceMetric.delta);
    const auto missingResultMetric = Compare(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(std::nullopt)));
    assert(missingResultMetric.classification ==
        Classification::MetricUnavailable);
    assert(!missingResultMetric.delta);

    const Metric unsupportedMetric(
        "infer_accuracy", 0.75, MetricSupport::Unsupported);
    const auto unsupported = Compare(source,
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            unsupportedMetric));
    assert(unsupported.classification == Classification::UnsupportedComparison);
    assert(!unsupported.delta);
    const auto overflowing = Compare(
        Source(501, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(-std::numeric_limits<double>::max())),
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(std::numeric_limits<double>::max())));
    assert(overflowing.classification ==
        Classification::UnsupportedComparison);
    assert(!overflowing.delta);

    std::vector<MemberEvidence> ordered;
    ordered.emplace_back(Member(1, Source(501), Result(901)));
    ordered.emplace_back(Member(2, Source(502), Result(902)));
    std::vector<MemberEvidence> shuffled;
    shuffled.emplace_back(Member(2, Source(502), Result(902)));
    shuffled.emplace_back(Member(1, Source(501), Result(901)));
    const auto firstAssessment = BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
        CampaignIdentity(), MaterializationIdentity(2),
        "2026-07-19T22:00:00Z", ordered);
    const auto repeatedAssessment =
        BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(CampaignIdentity(),
            MaterializationIdentity(2), "2026-07-19T22:00:00Z", ordered);
    const auto shuffledAssessment =
        BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(CampaignIdentity(),
            MaterializationIdentity(2), "2026-07-19T22:00:00Z", shuffled);
    assert(firstAssessment == repeatedAssessment);
    assert(firstAssessment == shuffledAssessment);
    assert(firstAssessment.members[0].identity.memberOrdinal == 1);
    assert(firstAssessment.members[1].identity.memberOrdinal == 2);
    assert(firstAssessment.identity.hash == RecommendationCanonicalHash(
        firstAssessment.identity.canonicalText));
    assert(firstAssessment.identity.canonicalText ==
        shuffledAssessment.identity.canonicalText);

    const auto laterObservation = BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
        CampaignIdentity(), MaterializationIdentity(2),
        "2026-07-19T22:05:00Z", shuffled);
    assert(laterObservation.observedAt != firstAssessment.observedAt);
    assert(laterObservation.identity == firstAssessment.identity);

    std::vector<MemberEvidence> matrix;
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(1, Source(501), Result(901)));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(2, Source(502),
        Result(902, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(0.70))));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(3, Source(503),
        Result(903, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(0.60))));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(4, Source(504),
        Result(904, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext("gbpusd"),
            NumericMetric(0.75))));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(5, std::nullopt, Result(905)));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(6, Source(506), std::nullopt));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(7, Source(507),
        Result(907, ResultState::NotTerminal)));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(8,
        Source(508, ComparableContext(), NumericMetric(std::nullopt)),
        Result(908)));
    matrix.emplace_back(Member(9, Source(509),
        Result(909, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            unsupportedMetric)));
    const auto matrixAssessment = BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
        CampaignIdentity(), MaterializationIdentity(9),
        "2026-07-19T22:10:00Z", matrix);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.memberCount == 9);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.comparableCount == 3);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.contextChangedCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.missingSourceEvidenceCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.missingResultEvidenceCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.resultNotTerminalCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.metricUnavailableCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.unsupportedComparisonCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.positiveDeltaCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.zeroDeltaCount == 1);
    assert(matrixAssessment.summary.negativeDeltaCount == 1);
    for (const auto& member : matrixAssessment.members)
    {
        if (member.comparison.classification == Classification::Comparable)
            assert(member.comparison.delta);
        else
            assert(!member.comparison.delta);
    }

    std::vector<MemberEvidence> changedValueMatrix;
    changedValueMatrix.emplace_back(Member(1, Source(501),
        Result(901, ResultState::Terminal, ComparableContext(),
            NumericMetric(0.76))));
    const auto changedValueAssessment =
        BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(CampaignIdentity(),
            MaterializationIdentity(1), "2026-07-19T22:10:00Z",
            changedValueMatrix);
    const auto originalValueAssessment =
        BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(CampaignIdentity(),
            MaterializationIdentity(1), "2026-07-19T22:10:00Z",
            {Member(1, Source(501), Result(901))});
    assert(changedValueAssessment.identity.hash !=
        originalValueAssessment.identity.hash);

    AssertInvalidArgument([]
    {
        (void)Metric("infer_accuracy",
            std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN(),
            MetricSupport::NumericDelta);
    });
    AssertInvalidArgument([]
    {
        (void)ComparableContext("eurusd", 12,
            std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());
    });
    AssertInvalidArgument([]
    {
        (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison(
            Classification::ContextChanged, 0.1,
            {ContextDifference::Symbol});
    });
    AssertInvalidArgument([]
    {
        (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison(
            Classification::Comparable, std::nullopt, {});
    });

    AssertInvalidArgument([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(CampaignIdentity(),
            MaterializationIdentity(2), "2026-07-19T22:00:00Z",
            {Member(1, Source(501), Result(901)),
                Member(1, Source(501), Result(902))});
    });
    AssertInvalidArgument([&]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(CampaignIdentity(),
            MaterializationIdentity(1), "2026-07-19T22:00:00Z",
            {Member(1, Source(999), Result(901))});
    });

    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
               Classification::Comparable) == "comparable");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
               Classification::ContextChanged) == "context_changed");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
               Classification::MissingSourceEvidence) ==
        "missing_source_evidence");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
               Classification::MissingResultEvidence) ==
        "missing_result_evidence");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
               Classification::ResultNotTerminal) == "result_not_terminal");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
               Classification::MetricUnavailable) == "metric_unavailable");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
               Classification::UnsupportedComparison) ==
        "unsupported_comparison");

    return 0;

exec

#pragma once

#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation
{

inline constexpr int
    kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion = 1;
inline constexpr int
    kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers = 1000;

// These immutable value types describe a read-only, point-in-time,
// non-persistent, and non-authoritative evidence assessment. They intentionally
// contain no campaign success decision or follow-up authorization.
struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity
{
    const long long campaignApprovalId;
    const std::string identityCanonical;
    const std::string identityHash;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity(
        long long campaignApprovalId,
        std::string identityCanonical,
        std::string identityHash);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&) const =
        default;
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity
{
    const long long materializationId;
    const long long campaignApprovalId;
    const int contractVersion;
    const int memberCount;
    const std::string identityCanonical;
    const std::string identityHash;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity(
        long long materializationId,
        long long campaignApprovalId,
        int contractVersion,
        int memberCount,
        std::string identityCanonical,
        std::string identityHash);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&)
        const = default;
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity
{
    const int memberOrdinal;
    const long long materializationMemberId;
    const long long rankingMemberId;
    const long long recommendationId;
    const long long sourceExperimentId;
    const long long proposalId;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity(
        int memberOrdinal,
        long long materializationMemberId,
        long long rankingMemberId,
        long long recommendationId,
        long long sourceExperimentId,
        long long proposalId);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity&) const =
        default;
};

enum class RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport
{
    NumericDelta,
    Unsupported
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric
{
    const std::string name;
    const std::optional<double> value;
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport
        comparisonSupport;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric(
        std::string name,
        std::optional<double> value,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport
            comparisonSupport);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric&) const = default;
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext
{
    const std::string symbol;
    const int predictionHorizon;
    const double threshold;
    const int windowSize;
    const std::string labelDefinition;
    const std::string inferenceRangeStart;
    const std::string inferenceRangeEnd;

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext(
        std::string symbol,
        int predictionHorizon,
        double threshold,
        int windowSize,
        std::string labelDefinition,
        std::string inferenceRangeStart,
        std::string inferenceRangeEnd);
    bool operator==(
        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext&) const =
        default;
};

struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence
{

        default;

codex
The contract shape already exposes two likely architectural blockers: each source/result evidence object carries a single `metric`, and result lifecycle is only `NotTerminal` versus `Terminal`. I’m now checking the construction invariants, canonical identity, summary semantics, surrounding Phase 5 contracts, and any written Step 5a/5b requirements to determine whether those are deliberate boundaries or unintended loss of information.

#include "ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp"

#include "ExperimentRecommendation.hpp"
#include "ExperimentRecommendationCampaignMaterialization.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <locale>
#include <set>
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <tuple>
#include <utility>

namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation

namespace

void RequireText(const std::string& value, const char* error)
{
    if (value.empty() || value.find('\0') != std::string::npos)
        throw std::invalid_argument(error);
}

void RequireOptionalPositive(
    const std::optional<long long>& value,
    const char* error)
{
    if (value && *value <= 0) throw std::invalid_argument(error);
}

std::string LengthPrefixed(const std::string& value)
{
    return std::to_string(value.size()) + ":" + value;
}

std::string OptionalLongLong(const std::optional<long long>& value)
{
    return value ? std::to_string(*value) : "null";
}

std::string OptionalDouble(const std::optional<double>& value)
{
    return value ? CanonicalRecommendationDouble(*value) : "null";
}

void AppendContext(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext& context)
{
    out << LengthPrefixed(context.symbol) << ',' << context.predictionHorizon
        << ',' << CanonicalRecommendationDouble(context.threshold) << ','
        << context.windowSize << ',' << LengthPrefixed(context.labelDefinition)
        << ',' << LengthPrefixed(context.inferenceRangeStart) << ','
        << LengthPrefixed(context.inferenceRangeEnd);
}

void AppendMetric(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric& metric)
{
    out << LengthPrefixed(metric.name) << ',' << OptionalDouble(metric.value)
        << ','
        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupportText(
               metric.comparisonSupport);
}

void AppendSourceEvidence(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>&
        evidence)
{
    if (!evidence)
    {
        out << "absent";
        return;
    }
    out << "present," << evidence->sourceExperimentId << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->sourceModelId) << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->sourceAnalysisId) << ',';
    AppendContext(out, evidence->context);
    out << ',';
    AppendMetric(out, evidence->metric);
}

void AppendResultEvidence(
    std::ostringstream& out,
    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>&
        evidence)
{
    if (!evidence)
    {
        out << "absent";
        return;
    }
    out << "present," << evidence->experimentId << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->modelId) << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->inferenceResultId) << ','
        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->analysisResultId) << ','
        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultStateText(
               evidence->state)
        << ',';
    AppendContext(out, evidence->context);
    out << ',';
    AppendMetric(out, evidence->metric);
}

std::string AssessmentCanonicalText(
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity& campaign,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
        materialization,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary& summary,
    const std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember>& members)
{
    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << "experiment_recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_v1"
        << ";assessment_contract_version="
        << kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion
        << ";campaign_approval_id=" << campaign.campaignApprovalId
        << ";campaign_identity_hash="
        << LengthPrefixed(campaign.identityHash)
        << ";materialization_id=" << materialization.materializationId
        << ";materialization_contract_version="
        << materialization.contractVersion
        << ";materialization_identity_hash="
        << LengthPrefixed(materialization.identityHash)
        << ";member_count=" << summary.memberCount;
    for (const auto& member : members)
    {
        out << ";member=" << member.identity.memberOrdinal << ','
            << member.identity.materializationMemberId << ','
            << member.identity.rankingMemberId << ','
            << member.identity.recommendationId << ','
            << member.identity.sourceExperimentId << ','
            << member.identity.proposalId << ",source=";
        AppendSourceEvidence(out, member.sourceEvidence);
        out << ",result=";
        AppendResultEvidence(out, member.resultEvidence);
        out << ",classification="
            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
                   member.comparison.classification)
            << ",delta=" << OptionalDouble(member.comparison.delta)
            << ",context_difference_count="
            << member.comparison.contextDifferences.size();
        for (const auto difference : member.comparison.contextDifferences)
            out << ','
                << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifferenceText(
                       difference);
    }
    out << ";comparable_count=" << summary.comparableCount
        << ";context_changed_count=" << summary.contextChangedCount
        << ";missing_source_evidence_count="
        << summary.missingSourceEvidenceCount
        << ";missing_result_evidence_count="
        << summary.missingResultEvidenceCount
        << ";result_not_terminal_count=" << summary.resultNotTerminalCount
        << ";metric_unavailable_count=" << summary.metricUnavailableCount
        << ";unsupported_comparison_count="
        << summary.unsupportedComparisonCount
        << ";positive_delta_count=" << summary.positiveDeltaCount
        << ";zero_delta_count=" << summary.zeroDeltaCount
        << ";negative_delta_count=" << summary.negativeDeltaCount;
    return out.str();
}

struct MutableSummary
{
    int comparableCount = 0;
    int contextChangedCount = 0;
    int missingSourceEvidenceCount = 0;
    int missingResultEvidenceCount = 0;
    int resultNotTerminalCount = 0;
    int metricUnavailableCount = 0;
    int unsupportedComparisonCount = 0;
    int positiveDeltaCount = 0;
    int zeroDeltaCount = 0;
    int negativeDeltaCount = 0;
};

void CountComparison(
    MutableSummary& summary,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison& comparison)
{
    switch (comparison.classification)
    {
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            Comparable:
            ++summary.comparableCount;
            if (*comparison.delta > 0.0)
                ++summary.positiveDeltaCount;
            else if (*comparison.delta < 0.0)
                ++summary.negativeDeltaCount;
            else
                ++summary.zeroDeltaCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            ContextChanged:
            ++summary.contextChangedCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            MissingSourceEvidence:
            ++summary.missingSourceEvidenceCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            MissingResultEvidence:
            ++summary.missingResultEvidenceCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            ResultNotTerminal:
            ++summary.resultNotTerminalCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            MetricUnavailable:
            ++summary.metricUnavailableCount;
            break;
        case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
            UnsupportedComparison:
            ++summary.unsupportedComparisonCount;
            break;
    }
}

} // namespace

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity(
        long long campaignApprovalIdValue,
        std::string identityCanonicalValue,
        std::string identityHashValue)
    : campaignApprovalId(campaignApprovalIdValue),
      identityCanonical(std::move(identityCanonicalValue)),
      identityHash(std::move(identityHashValue))
{
    if (campaignApprovalId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_id_invalid");
    RequireText(identityCanonical,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_missing");
    if (identityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(identityCanonical))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity(
        long long materializationIdValue,
        long long campaignApprovalIdValue,
        int contractVersionValue,
        int memberCountValue,
        std::string identityCanonicalValue,
        std::string identityHashValue)
    : materializationId(materializationIdValue),
      campaignApprovalId(campaignApprovalIdValue),
      contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
      memberCount(memberCountValue),
      identityCanonical(std::move(identityCanonicalValue)),
      identityHash(std::move(identityHashValue))
{
    if (materializationId <= 0 || campaignApprovalId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_id_invalid");
    if (memberCount <= 0 ||
        memberCount > kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_member_count_invalid");
    RequireText(identityCanonical,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_identity_missing");
    if (identityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(identityCanonical))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_identity_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity(
        int memberOrdinalValue,
        long long materializationMemberIdValue,
        long long rankingMemberIdValue,
        long long recommendationIdValue,
        long long sourceExperimentIdValue,
        long long proposalIdValue)
    : memberOrdinal(memberOrdinalValue),
      materializationMemberId(materializationMemberIdValue),
      rankingMemberId(rankingMemberIdValue),
      recommendationId(recommendationIdValue),
      sourceExperimentId(sourceExperimentIdValue),
      proposalId(proposalIdValue)
{
    if (memberOrdinal <= 0 || materializationMemberId <= 0 ||
        rankingMemberId <= 0 || recommendationId <= 0 ||
        sourceExperimentId <= 0 || proposalId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_identity_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric(
        std::string nameValue,
        std::optional<double> valueValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport
            comparisonSupportValue)
    : name(std::move(nameValue)),
      value(valueValue),
      comparisonSupport(comparisonSupportValue)
{
    RequireText(name,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_metric_name_invalid");
    if (value && !std::isfinite(*value))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_metric_value_nonfinite");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext(
        std::string symbolValue,
        int predictionHorizonValue,
        double thresholdValue,
        int windowSizeValue,
        std::string labelDefinitionValue,
        std::string inferenceRangeStartValue,
        std::string inferenceRangeEndValue)
    : symbol(std::move(symbolValue)),
      predictionHorizon(predictionHorizonValue),
      threshold(thresholdValue == 0.0 ? 0.0 : thresholdValue),
      windowSize(windowSizeValue),
      labelDefinition(std::move(labelDefinitionValue)),
      inferenceRangeStart(std::move(inferenceRangeStartValue)),
      inferenceRangeEnd(std::move(inferenceRangeEndValue))
{
    RequireText(symbol,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_symbol_invalid");
    RequireText(labelDefinition,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_label_definition_invalid");
    RequireText(inferenceRangeStart,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_inference_range_invalid");
    RequireText(inferenceRangeEnd,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_inference_range_invalid");
    if (predictionHorizon <= 0 || windowSize <= 0 || !std::isfinite(threshold))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence(
        long long sourceExperimentIdValue,
        std::optional<long long> sourceModelIdValue,
        std::optional<long long> sourceAnalysisIdValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext contextValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric metricValue)
    : sourceExperimentId(sourceExperimentIdValue),
      sourceModelId(sourceModelIdValue),
      sourceAnalysisId(sourceAnalysisIdValue),
      context(std::move(contextValue)),
      metric(std::move(metricValue))
{
    if (sourceExperimentId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_source_experiment_id_invalid");
    RequireOptionalPositive(sourceModelId,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_source_model_id_invalid");
    RequireOptionalPositive(sourceAnalysisId,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_source_analysis_id_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence(
        long long experimentIdValue,
        std::optional<long long> modelIdValue,
        std::optional<long long> inferenceResultIdValue,
        std::optional<long long> analysisResultIdValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState stateValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext contextValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetric metricValue)
    : experimentId(experimentIdValue),
      modelId(modelIdValue),
      inferenceResultId(inferenceResultIdValue),
      analysisResultId(analysisResultIdValue),
      state(stateValue),
      context(std::move(contextValue)),
      metric(std::move(metricValue))
{
    if (experimentId <= 0)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_result_experiment_id_invalid");
    RequireOptionalPositive(modelId,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_result_model_id_invalid");
    RequireOptionalPositive(inferenceResultId,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_inference_result_id_invalid");
    RequireOptionalPositive(analysisResultId,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_analysis_result_id_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence(
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity identityValue,
        std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>
            sourceEvidenceValue,
        std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>
            resultEvidenceValue)
    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
      sourceEvidence(std::move(sourceEvidenceValue)),
      resultEvidence(std::move(resultEvidenceValue))
{
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison(
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification
            classificationValue,
        std::optional<double> deltaValue,
        std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference>
            contextDifferencesValue)
    : classification(classificationValue),
      delta(deltaValue),
      contextDifferences(std::move(contextDifferencesValue))
{
    if (classification ==
            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
                Comparable)
    {
        if (!delta || !std::isfinite(*delta) || !contextDifferences.empty())
            throw std::invalid_argument(
                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparable_invalid");
    }
    else if (delta)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_noncomparable_delta_invalid");
    if (classification ==
            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
                ContextChanged &&
        contextDifferences.empty())
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_change_missing");
    if (classification !=
            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
                ContextChanged &&
        !contextDifferences.empty())
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_context_change_unexpected");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary(
        int memberCountValue,
        int comparableCountValue,
        int contextChangedCountValue,
        int missingSourceEvidenceCountValue,
        int missingResultEvidenceCountValue,
        int resultNotTerminalCountValue,
        int metricUnavailableCountValue,
        int unsupportedComparisonCountValue,
        int positiveDeltaCountValue,
        int zeroDeltaCountValue,
        int negativeDeltaCountValue)
    : memberCount(memberCountValue),
      comparableCount(comparableCountValue),
      contextChangedCount(contextChangedCountValue),
      missingSourceEvidenceCount(missingSourceEvidenceCountValue),
      missingResultEvidenceCount(missingResultEvidenceCountValue),
      resultNotTerminalCount(resultNotTerminalCountValue),
      metricUnavailableCount(metricUnavailableCountValue),
      unsupportedComparisonCount(unsupportedComparisonCountValue),
      positiveDeltaCount(positiveDeltaCountValue),
      zeroDeltaCount(zeroDeltaCountValue),
      negativeDeltaCount(negativeDeltaCountValue)
{
    const int classifiedCount = comparableCount + contextChangedCount +
        missingSourceEvidenceCount + missingResultEvidenceCount +
        resultNotTerminalCount + metricUnavailableCount +
        unsupportedComparisonCount;
    const int deltaCount =
        positiveDeltaCount + zeroDeltaCount + negativeDeltaCount;
    if (memberCount < 0 || comparableCount < 0 || contextChangedCount < 0 ||
        missingSourceEvidenceCount < 0 || missingResultEvidenceCount < 0 ||
        resultNotTerminalCount < 0 || metricUnavailableCount < 0 ||
        unsupportedComparisonCount < 0 || positiveDeltaCount < 0 ||
        zeroDeltaCount < 0 || negativeDeltaCount < 0 ||
        classifiedCount != memberCount || deltaCount != comparableCount)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_summary_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember(
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity identityValue,
        std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>
            sourceEvidenceValue,
        std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>
            resultEvidenceValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison comparisonValue)
    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
      sourceEvidence(std::move(sourceEvidenceValue)),
      resultEvidence(std::move(resultEvidenceValue)),
      comparison(std::move(comparisonValue))
{
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity(
        int contractVersionValue,
        std::string canonicalTextValue,
        std::string hashValue)
    : contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
      canonicalText(std::move(canonicalTextValue)),
      hash(std::move(hashValue))
{
    if (contractVersion !=
        kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_contract_version_unsupported");
    RequireText(canonicalText,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_identity_missing");
    if (hash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonicalText))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_identity_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity identityValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity
            campaignIdentityValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity
            materializationIdentityValue,
        std::string observedAtValue,
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary summaryValue,
        std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember> membersValue)
    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
      campaignIdentity(std::move(campaignIdentityValue)),
      materializationIdentity(std::move(materializationIdentityValue)),
      observedAt(std::move(observedAtValue)),
      summary(std::move(summaryValue)),
      members(std::move(membersValue))
{
    RequireText(observedAt,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison
CompareRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>&
        sourceEvidence,
    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>&
        resultEvidence)
{
    using Classification =
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification;
    using Difference =
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference;
    if (!sourceEvidence)
        return {Classification::MissingSourceEvidence, std::nullopt, {}};
    if (!resultEvidence)
        return {Classification::MissingResultEvidence, std::nullopt, {}};
    if (resultEvidence->state ==
        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState::NotTerminal)
        return {Classification::ResultNotTerminal, std::nullopt, {}};

    std::vector<Difference> differences;
    if (sourceEvidence->context.symbol != resultEvidence->context.symbol)
        differences.push_back(Difference::Symbol);
    if (sourceEvidence->context.predictionHorizon !=
        resultEvidence->context.predictionHorizon)
        differences.push_back(Difference::PredictionHorizon);
    if (sourceEvidence->context.threshold != resultEvidence->context.threshold)
        differences.push_back(Difference::Threshold);
    if (sourceEvidence->context.windowSize !=
        resultEvidence->context.windowSize)
        differences.push_back(Difference::WindowSize);
    if (sourceEvidence->context.labelDefinition !=
        resultEvidence->context.labelDefinition)
        differences.push_back(Difference::LabelDefinition);
    if (sourceEvidence->metric.name != resultEvidence->metric.name)
        differences.push_back(Difference::MetricIdentity);
    if (sourceEvidence->context.inferenceRangeStart !=
            resultEvidence->context.inferenceRangeStart ||
        sourceEvidence->context.inferenceRangeEnd !=
            resultEvidence->context.inferenceRangeEnd)
        differences.push_back(Difference::InferenceRange);
    if (!differences.empty())
        return {Classification::ContextChanged, std::nullopt,
            std::move(differences)};

    if (!sourceEvidence->metric.value || !resultEvidence->metric.value)
        return {Classification::MetricUnavailable, std::nullopt, {}};
    if (sourceEvidence->metric.comparisonSupport !=
            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport::
                NumericDelta ||
        resultEvidence->metric.comparisonSupport !=
            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport::
                NumericDelta)
        return {Classification::UnsupportedComparison, std::nullopt, {}};

    double delta =
        *resultEvidence->metric.value - *sourceEvidence->metric.value;
    if (!std::isfinite(delta))
        return {Classification::UnsupportedComparison, std::nullopt, {}};
    if (delta == 0.0) delta = 0.0;
    return {Classification::Comparable, delta, {}};
}

RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&
        campaignIdentity,
    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
        materializationIdentity,
    const std::string& observedAt,
    const std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence>&
        members)
{
    RequireText(observedAt,
        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
    if (materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId !=
        campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_materialization_mismatch");
    if (materializationIdentity.contractVersion !=
        kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationContractVersion)
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_version_unsupported");
    if (materializationIdentity.memberCount !=
        static_cast<int>(members.size()))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_mismatch");
    if (members.empty() ||
        members.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(
                             kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers))
        throw std::invalid_argument(
            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_invalid");

    std::vector<const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence*>
        ordered;
    ordered.reserve(members.size());
    for (const auto& member : members) ordered.push_back(&member);
    std::sort(ordered.begin(), ordered.end(), [](const auto* left, const auto* right)
    {
        return std::tie(left->identity.memberOrdinal,
                   left->identity.materializationMemberId,
                   left->identity.rankingMemberId,
                   left->identity.recommendationId,
                   left->identity.proposalId) <
            std::tie(right->identity.memberOrdinal,
                   right->identity.materializationMemberId,
                   right->identity.rankingMemberId,
                   right->identity.recommendationId,
                   right->identity.proposalId);
    });

    std::set<long long> materializationMemberIds;
    std::set<long long> rankingMemberIds;
    std::set<long long> recommendationIds;
    std::set<long long> proposalIds;
    MutableSummary counts;
    std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember> assessments;
    assessments.reserve(ordered.size());
    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < ordered.size(); ++index)
    {
        const auto& member = *ordered[index];
        if (member.identity.memberOrdinal != static_cast<int>(index) + 1 ||
            !materializationMemberIds
                 .insert(member.identity.materializationMemberId)
                 .second ||
            !rankingMemberIds.insert(member.identity.rankingMemberId).second ||
            !recommendationIds.insert(member.identity.recommendationId).second ||
            !proposalIds.insert(member.identity.proposalId).second)
            throw std::invalid_argument(
                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_identity_set_invalid");
        if (member.sourceEvidence &&
            member.sourceEvidence->sourceExperimentId !=
                member.identity.sourceExperimentId)
            throw std::invalid_argument(
                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_source_provenance_mismatch");
        auto comparison = CompareRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
            member.sourceEvidence, member.resultEvidence);
        CountComparison(counts, comparison);
        assessments.emplace_back(member.identity, member.sourceEvidence,
            member.resultEvidence, std::move(comparison));
    }

    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary summary(
        static_cast<int>(assessments.size()), counts.comparableCount,
        counts.contextChangedCount, counts.missingSourceEvidenceCount,
        counts.missingResultEvidenceCount, counts.resultNotTerminalCount,
        counts.metricUnavailableCount, counts.unsupportedComparisonCount,
        counts.positiveDeltaCount, counts.zeroDeltaCount,
        counts.negativeDeltaCount);
    const std::string canonical = AssessmentCanonicalText(campaignIdentity,
        materializationIdentity, summary, assessments);
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity assessmentIdentity(
        kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion, canonical,
        RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonical));
    return {std::move(assessmentIdentity), campaignIdentity,
        materializationIdentity, observedAt, std::move(summary),
        std::move(assessments)};
}

#define EA_RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_TEXT_FUNCTION(          \
    name, type, cases)                                                        \
    std::string name(type value)                                              \
    {                                                                         \
        switch (value)                                                        \
        {                                                                     \
            cases                                                             \
        }                                                                     \
        throw std::invalid_argument(#name "_invalid");                       \
    }

EA_RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_TEXT_FUNCTION(
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupportText,
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport,
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport::
        NumericDelta: return "numeric_delta";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport::
        Unsupported: return "unsupported";)

EA_RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_TEXT_FUNCTION(
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultStateText,
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState,
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState::NotTerminal:
        return "not_terminal";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState::Terminal:
        return "terminal";)

EA_RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_TEXT_FUNCTION(
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText,
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification,
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
        Comparable: return "comparable";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
        ContextChanged: return "context_changed";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
        MissingSourceEvidence: return "missing_source_evidence";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
        MissingResultEvidence: return "missing_result_evidence";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
        ResultNotTerminal: return "result_not_terminal";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
        MetricUnavailable: return "metric_unavailable";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification::
        UnsupportedComparison: return "unsupported_comparison";)

EA_RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_TEXT_FUNCTION(
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifferenceText,
    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference,
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference::Symbol:
        return "symbol";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference::
        PredictionHorizon: return "prediction_horizon";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference::Threshold:
        return "threshold";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference::WindowSize:
        return "window_size";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference::
        LabelDefinition: return "label_definition";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference::MetricIdentity:
        return "metric_identity";
    case RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference::InferenceRange:
        return "inference_range";)

#undef EA_RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_TEXT_FUNCTION
