 20920	Contract rules:
 20921	
 20922	- One positive materialization ID is required.
 20923	- Duplicate forms and combinations with any other standalone operation are rejected before database dispatch.
 20924	- There are no filters and no list command in Step 5; this prevents broad scans.
 20925	- `--yes` is rejected because the command is read-only.
 20926	- `--dry-run` is rejected because the command is intrinsically a dry read.
 20927	- No operator, reason, score threshold, or profitability option is accepted.
 20928	
 20929	### Membership authority
 20930	
 20931	Membership is exactly the stored one-based ordinal sequence in:
 20932	
 20933	- `experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization`; and
 20934	- `experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization_member`.
 20935	
 20936	The existing materialization validator must establish:
 20937	
 20938	- supported contract version;
 20939	- valid canonical/hash pair;
 20940	- positive member count of at most 1000;
 20941	- count agreement;
 20942	- contiguous ordinals;
 20943	- unique member, ranking-member, and proposal links; and
 20944	- exact recommendation, source-experiment, and proposal provenance.
 20945	
 20946	Continuation children, replacement experiments, semantic duplicates, current ranking output, and inferred proposal matches are excluded.
 20947	
 20948	### Eligibility and evidence rules
 20949	
 20950	The inspection command is valid at any campaign progress state. Research metrics are available for a member only when:
 20951	
 20952	1. Step 4 classifies its downstream evidence as consistent;
 20953	2. it is terminal `completed/done`;
 20954	3. it has exactly one current model linked to the experiment;
 20955	4. it has exactly one completed final inference row matching current model, symbol, horizon, threshold, inference dates, final scope, and null checkpoint linkage;
 20956	5. it has exactly one completed final analysis row matching experiment, current model, and final scope;
 20957	6. the materialized recommendation and proposal still match their stored provenance; and
 20958	7. required numeric values are finite.
 20959	
 20960	The frozen source baseline comes from the persisted recommendation, not by reopening and recomputing the source experiment:
 20961	
 20962	- source experiment/model/analysis IDs;
 20963	- source leader score;
 20964	- source inference accuracy;
 20965	- optional source predicted-neutral proportion;
 20966	- source evidence count;
 20967	- changed parameter;
 20968	- source and proposed canonical values; and
 20969	- source/proposed invocation identities.
 20970	
 20971	The observed result side reports:
 20972	
 20973	- campaign experiment and model IDs;
 20974	- exact final inference-result ID and accuracy;
 20975	- exact final analysis ID;
 20976	- leader score;
 20977	- inference accuracy;
 20978	- derived predicted-neutral proportion and evidence count; and
 20979	- result timestamps and identity/provenance fields.
 20980	
 20981	### Comparison context
 20982	
 20983	Each successful member is classified as:
 20984	
 20985	- `same_evaluation_context` when source and proposed symbol, horizon, label threshold, and inference date range match; or
 20986	- `changed_evaluation_context` otherwise.
 20987	
 20988	Raw source and outcome metrics remain visible in both cases. Leader-score and inference-accuracy delta classifications are included in campaign aggregates only for `same_evaluation_context`; Step 5 does not pretend cross-horizon or cross-threshold values are directly comparable.
 20989	
 20990	### Per-member outcomes
 20991	
 20992	Each member has exactly one outcome:
 20993	
 20994	- `not_ready`: proposal-only, paused, pending, running, or paused after progress;
 20995	- `succeeded_comparable`: completed with complete metrics and the same evaluation context;
 20996	- `succeeded_context_changed`: completed with metrics but changed evaluation context;
 20997	- `succeeded_metric_gap`: valid completed lifecycle/result rows but leader score or inference accuracy is unavailable;
 20998	- `terminal_failed`: valid failed lifecycle;
 20999	- `terminal_cancelled`: valid cancelled lifecycle; or
 21000	- `inconsistent`: invalid membership-adjacent or downstream provenance/cardinality/lifecycle/result evidence.
 21001	
 21002	A failed or cancelled member is an observed research outcome, not an integrity error.
 21003	
 21004	### Aggregate outcome precedence
 21005	
 21006	1. Any inconsistent member: `inconsistent`.
 21007	2. Any nonterminal member: `not_ready`.
 21008	3. All cancelled: `cancelled`.
 21009	4. All succeeded with complete metrics: `completed`.
 21010	5. All succeeded but at least one metric gap: `completed_with_metric_gaps`.
 21011	6. All terminal, with at least one failure and no cancellation: `completed_with_failures`.
 21012	7. Any other all-terminal mixture: `mixed_terminal`.
 21013	
 21014	The aggregate also reports comparable/context-changed/metric-gap counts and positive/zero/negative delta counts for leader score and inference accuracy. It does not compute a campaign score, weighted mean, statistical significance, profitability, or authorization decision.
 21015	
 21016	### Identity and retry contract
 21017	
 21018	Outcome contract version 1 canonically binds:
 21019	
 21020	- materialization ID, version, identity, and ordered membership;
 21021	- the Step 4 status snapshot identity;
 21022	- frozen recommendation source evidence;
 21023	- proposal mutation and invocation evidence;
 21024	- exact result IDs, statuses, timestamps, and metric values;
 21025	- per-member outcome, comparison context, and diagnostics; and
 21026	- aggregate counts.
 21027	
 21028	`observed_at`, local time, process ID, transaction ID, and row-return order are excluded.
 21029	
 21030	An exact retry over unchanged durable evidence produces the same outcome identity hash. If scheduler state or result evidence changes, the command produces a new point-in-time identity. No duplicate row can be created because nothing is persisted.
 21031	
 21032	### Explicit non-goals
 21033	
 21034	Step 5 does not:
 21035	
 21036	- approve, reject, execute, activate, pause, resume, cancel, retry, stop, or repair anything;
 21037	- persist a completion or outcome row;
 21038	- close a campaign or prevent later experiment retries;
 21039	- follow continuation or lineage children;
 21040	- query process tables or infer liveness;

   344	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassificationText(
   345	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification value);
   346	std::string RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifferenceText(
   347	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference value);
   348	
   349	} // namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation
   519	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::
   520	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
   521	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity identityValue,
   522	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity
   523	            campaignIdentityValue,
   524	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity
   525	            materializationIdentityValue,
   526	        std::string observedAtValue,
   527	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary summaryValue,
   528	        std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember> membersValue)
   529	    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
   530	      campaignIdentity(std::move(campaignIdentityValue)),
   531	      materializationIdentity(std::move(materializationIdentityValue)),
   532	      observedAt(std::move(observedAtValue)),
   533	      summary(std::move(summaryValue)),
   534	      members(std::move(membersValue))
   535	{
   536	    RequireText(observedAt,
   537	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
   538	}
   539	
   540	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparison
   541	CompareRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   542	    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>&
   543	        sourceEvidence,
   544	    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>&
   545	        resultEvidence)
   546	{
   547	    using Classification =
   548	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonClassification;
   549	    using Difference =
   550	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifference;
   551	    if (!sourceEvidence)
   552	        return {Classification::MissingSourceEvidence, std::nullopt, {}};
   553	    if (!resultEvidence)
   554	        return {Classification::MissingResultEvidence, std::nullopt, {}};
   555	    if (resultEvidence->state ==
   556	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState::NotTerminal)
   557	        return {Classification::ResultNotTerminal, std::nullopt, {}};
   558	
   559	    std::vector<Difference> differences;
   560	    if (sourceEvidence->context.symbol != resultEvidence->context.symbol)
   561	        differences.push_back(Difference::Symbol);
   562	    if (sourceEvidence->context.predictionHorizon !=
   563	        resultEvidence->context.predictionHorizon)
   564	        differences.push_back(Difference::PredictionHorizon);
   565	    if (sourceEvidence->context.threshold != resultEvidence->context.threshold)
   566	        differences.push_back(Difference::Threshold);
   567	    if (sourceEvidence->context.windowSize !=
   568	        resultEvidence->context.windowSize)
   569	        differences.push_back(Difference::WindowSize);
   570	    if (sourceEvidence->context.labelDefinition !=
   571	        resultEvidence->context.labelDefinition)
   572	        differences.push_back(Difference::LabelDefinition);
   573	    if (sourceEvidence->metric.name != resultEvidence->metric.name)
   574	        differences.push_back(Difference::MetricIdentity);
   575	    if (sourceEvidence->context.inferenceRangeStart !=
   576	            resultEvidence->context.inferenceRangeStart ||
   577	        sourceEvidence->context.inferenceRangeEnd !=
   578	            resultEvidence->context.inferenceRangeEnd)
   579	        differences.push_back(Difference::InferenceRange);
   580	    if (!differences.empty())
   581	        return {Classification::ContextChanged, std::nullopt,
   582	            std::move(differences)};
   583	
   584	    if (!sourceEvidence->metric.value || !resultEvidence->metric.value)
   585	        return {Classification::MetricUnavailable, std::nullopt, {}};
   586	    if (sourceEvidence->metric.comparisonSupport !=
   587	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport::
   588	                NumericDelta ||
   589	        resultEvidence->metric.comparisonSupport !=
   590	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupport::
   591	                NumericDelta)
   592	        return {Classification::UnsupportedComparison, std::nullopt, {}};
   593	
   594	    double delta =
   595	        *resultEvidence->metric.value - *sourceEvidence->metric.value;
   596	    if (!std::isfinite(delta))
   597	        return {Classification::UnsupportedComparison, std::nullopt, {}};
   598	    if (delta == 0.0) delta = 0.0;
   599	    return {Classification::Comparable, delta, {}};
   600	}
   601	
   602	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
   603	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&
   604	        campaignIdentity,
   605	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
   606	        materializationIdentity,
   607	    const std::string& observedAt,
   608	    const std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence>&
   609	        members)
   610	{
   611	    RequireText(observedAt,
   612	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
   613	    if (materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId !=
   614	        campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId)
   615	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   616	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_materialization_mismatch");
   617	    if (materializationIdentity.contractVersion !=
   618	        kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationContractVersion)
   619	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   620	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_version_unsupported");
   621	    if (materializationIdentity.memberCount !=
   622	        static_cast<int>(members.size()))
   623	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   624	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_mismatch");
   625	    if (members.empty() ||
   626	        members.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(
   627	                             kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers))
   628	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   629	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_invalid");
   630	
   631	    std::vector<const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence*>
   632	        ordered;
   633	    ordered.reserve(members.size());
   634	    for (const auto& member : members) ordered.push_back(&member);
   635	    std::sort(ordered.begin(), ordered.end(), [](const auto* left, const auto* right)
   636	    {
   637	        return std::tie(left->identity.memberOrdinal,
   638	                   left->identity.materializationMemberId,
   639	                   left->identity.rankingMemberId,
   640	                   left->identity.recommendationId,
   641	                   left->identity.proposalId) <
   642	            std::tie(right->identity.memberOrdinal,
   643	                   right->identity.materializationMemberId,
   644	                   right->identity.rankingMemberId,
   645	                   right->identity.recommendationId,
   646	                   right->identity.proposalId);
   647	    });
   648	
   649	    std::set<long long> materializationMemberIds;
   650	    std::set<long long> rankingMemberIds;
   651	    std::set<long long> recommendationIds;
   652	    std::set<long long> proposalIds;
   653	    MutableSummary counts;
   654	    std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember> assessments;
   655	    assessments.reserve(ordered.size());
   656	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < ordered.size(); ++index)
   657	    {
   658	        const auto& member = *ordered[index];
   659	        if (member.identity.memberOrdinal != static_cast<int>(index) + 1 ||
   660	            !materializationMemberIds
   661	                 .insert(member.identity.materializationMemberId)
   662	                 .second ||
   663	            !rankingMemberIds.insert(member.identity.rankingMemberId).second ||
   664	            !recommendationIds.insert(member.identity.recommendationId).second ||
   665	            !proposalIds.insert(member.identity.proposalId).second)
   666	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   667	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_identity_set_invalid");
   668	        if (member.sourceEvidence &&
   669	            member.sourceEvidence->sourceExperimentId !=
   670	                member.identity.sourceExperimentId)
   671	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   672	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_source_provenance_mismatch");
   673	        auto comparison = CompareRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   674	            member.sourceEvidence, member.resultEvidence);
   675	        CountComparison(counts, comparison);
   676	        assessments.emplace_back(member.identity, member.sourceEvidence,
   677	            member.resultEvidence, std::move(comparison));
   678	    }
   679	
   680	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary summary(
   681	        static_cast<int>(assessments.size()), counts.comparableCount,
   682	        counts.contextChangedCount, counts.missingSourceEvidenceCount,
   683	        counts.missingResultEvidenceCount, counts.resultNotTerminalCount,
   684	        counts.metricUnavailableCount, counts.unsupportedComparisonCount,
   685	        counts.positiveDeltaCount, counts.zeroDeltaCount,
   686	        counts.negativeDeltaCount);
   687	    const std::string canonical = AssessmentCanonicalText(campaignIdentity,
   688	        materializationIdentity, summary, assessments);
   689	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity assessmentIdentity(
   690	        kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion, canonical,
   691	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonical));
   692	    return {std::move(assessmentIdentity), campaignIdentity,
   693	        materializationIdentity, observedAt, std::move(summary),
   694	        std::move(assessments)};
   695	}
   696	
   697	#define EA_RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_TEXT_FUNCTION(          \
   698	    name, type, cases)                                                        \
   699	    std::string name(type value)                                              \
   700	    {                                                                         \
   701	        switch (value)                                                        \
   702	        {                                                                     \
   703	            cases                                                             \
   704	        }                                                                     \
   705	        throw std::invalid_argument(#name "_invalid");                       \
    15	{
    16	
    17	void Diagnose(RecommendationCampaignStatusMember& member, std::string code)
    18	{
    19	    if (std::find(member.diagnosticCodes.begin(), member.diagnosticCodes.end(), code) ==
    20	        member.diagnosticCodes.end())
    21	        member.diagnosticCodes.push_back(std::move(code));
    22	    member.consistency = RecommendationCampaignStatusConsistency::inconsistent;
    23	    member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::inconsistent;
    24	    member.terminalResult = RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::unknown;
    25	}
    26	
    27	bool IsKnownStatus(const std::string& value)
    28	{
    29	    return value == "pending" || value == "paused" || value == "running" ||
    30	        value == "completed" || value == "failed" || value == "cancelled";
    31	}
    32	
    33	bool IsKnownPhase(const std::string& value)
    34	{
    35	    return value == "train" || value == "infer" || value == "analyze" ||
    36	        value == "done";
    37	}
    38	
    39	RecommendationCampaignStatusMember ClassifyMember(
    40	    const RecommendationCampaignStatusMemberInput& input)
    41	{
    42	    RecommendationCampaignStatusMember member;
    43	    member.memberOrdinal = input.memberOrdinal;
    44	    member.materializationMemberId = input.materializationMemberId;
    45	    member.recommendationId = input.recommendationId;
    46	    member.sourceExperimentId = input.sourceExperimentId;
    47	    member.rankingMemberId = input.rankingMemberId;
    48	    member.proposalId = input.proposalId;
    49	    member.currentReviewDecisionId = input.currentReviewDecisionId;
    50	    member.currentReviewDecision = input.currentReviewDecision;
    51	    member.authorizationReviewDecisionId = input.authorizationReviewDecisionId;
    52	    member.executionId = input.executionId;
    53	    member.activationId = input.activationId;
    54	    member.experimentId = input.experimentId;
    55	    member.executionEvidenceCount = input.executionCount;
    56	    member.activationEvidenceCount = input.activationCount;
    57	
    58	    if (input.executionCount > 1)
    59	        member.executionState = RecommendationCampaignStatusExecutionState::conflicting;
    60	    else if (input.executionId)
    61	        member.executionState = RecommendationCampaignStatusExecutionState::executed;
    62	    if (input.activationCount > 1)
    63	        member.activationState = RecommendationCampaignStatusActivationState::conflicting;
    64	    else if (input.activationId)
    65	        member.activationState = RecommendationCampaignStatusActivationState::activated;
    66	
    67	    if (!input.workflowConsistent)
    68	    {
    69	        if (input.executionCount == 1 && input.executionId)
    70	            member.executionState = RecommendationCampaignStatusExecutionState::malformed;
    71	        if (input.activationCount == 1 && input.activationId)
    72	            member.activationState = RecommendationCampaignStatusActivationState::malformed;
    73	        for (const auto& code : input.workflowDiagnostics) Diagnose(member, code);
    74	        if (input.workflowDiagnostics.empty())
    75	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_workflow_inconsistent");
    76	    }
    77	    if (input.executionCount < 0 || input.executionCount > 1 ||
    78	        (input.executionId.has_value() != (input.executionCount == 1)))
    79	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_execution_cardinality_invalid");
    80	    if (input.activationCount < 0 || input.activationCount > 1 ||
    81	        (input.activationId.has_value() != (input.activationCount == 1)))
    82	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_activation_cardinality_invalid");
    83	    if (input.activationId && !input.executionId)
    84	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_activation_without_execution");
    85	    if (!input.executionId)
    86	    {
    87	        if (input.experiment || input.experimentId)
    88	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_experiment_without_execution");
    89	        return member;
    90	    }
    91	    if (!input.experiment || !input.experimentId)
    92	    {
    93	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_execution_experiment_missing");
    94	        return member;
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    const auto& experiment = *input.experiment;
    98	    member.symbol = experiment.symbol;
    99	    member.predictionHorizon = experiment.predictionHorizon;
   100	    member.status = experiment.status;
   101	    member.phase = experiment.phase;
   102	    member.currentEpoch = experiment.currentEpoch;
   103	    member.targetEpochs = experiment.targetEpochs;
   104	    member.workerPid = experiment.workerPid;
   105	    member.currentOperation = experiment.currentOperation;
   106	    member.workerStartedAt = experiment.workerStartedAt;
   107	    member.startedAt = experiment.startedAt;
   108	    member.completedAt = experiment.completedAt;
   109	    member.exitCode = experiment.exitCode;
   110	    member.errorMessage = experiment.errorMessage;
   111	    member.modelId = experiment.modelId;
   112	    if (!experiment.invocationIdentityCanonical.empty())
   113	        member.invocationIdentityHash = RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   114	            experiment.invocationIdentityCanonical);
   115	    member.modelLinkCount = experiment.modelLinkCount;
   116	    member.finalInferenceResultCount = experiment.inferenceResultCount;
   117	    member.completedFinalInferenceResultCount =
   118	        experiment.completedInferenceResultCount;
   119	    member.failedFinalInferenceResultCount =
   120	        experiment.failedInferenceResultCount;
   121	    member.finalAnalysisResultCount = experiment.analysisResultCount;
   122	    member.completedFinalAnalysisResultCount =
   123	        experiment.completedAnalysisResultCount;
   124	    member.failedFinalAnalysisResultCount = experiment.failedAnalysisResultCount;
   125	    member.inferConfigured = experiment.inferConfigured;
   126	    member.analyzeConfigured = experiment.inferConfigured;
   127	    member.inferComplete = experiment.completedInferenceResultCount > 0;
   128	    member.analyzeComplete = experiment.completedAnalysisResultCount > 0;
   129	    member.trainComplete = experiment.modelId.has_value() &&
   130	        experiment.modelLinkCount == 1;
   131	
   132	    if (experiment.experimentId != *input.experimentId)
   133	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_experiment_provenance_mismatch");
   134	    if (!experiment.invocationProvenanceValid)
   135	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_experiment_invocation_mismatch");
   136	    if (experiment.symbol.empty() || experiment.predictionHorizon <= 0 ||
   137	        experiment.targetEpochs <= 0 || experiment.duplicateNonce != 0 ||
   138	        experiment.invocationMode != "recommendation_conversion")
   139	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_experiment_identity_invalid");
   140	    if (!IsKnownStatus(experiment.status))
   141	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_experiment_status_unknown");
   142	    if (!IsKnownPhase(experiment.phase))
   143	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_experiment_phase_unknown");
   144	    if (experiment.currentEpoch &&
   145	        (*experiment.currentEpoch < 0 || *experiment.currentEpoch > experiment.targetEpochs))
   146	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_current_epoch_invalid");
   147	    if (experiment.inferStartPresent != experiment.inferEndPresent)
   148	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_inference_configuration_invalid");
   149	    if (experiment.modelLinkCount < 0 || experiment.modelLinkCount > 1)
   150	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_model_link_conflict");
   151	    if (experiment.inferenceResultCount < 0 ||
   152	        experiment.completedInferenceResultCount < 0 ||
   153	        experiment.failedInferenceResultCount < 0 ||
   154	        experiment.completedInferenceResultCount +
   155	                experiment.failedInferenceResultCount !=
   156	            experiment.inferenceResultCount ||
   157	        experiment.completedInferenceResultCount > 1 ||
   158	        (experiment.completedInferenceResultCount > 0 &&
   159	         experiment.failedInferenceResultCount > 0))
   160	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_inference_evidence_invalid");
   161	    if (experiment.analysisResultCount < 0 ||
   162	        experiment.completedAnalysisResultCount < 0 ||
   163	        experiment.failedAnalysisResultCount < 0 ||
   164	        experiment.completedAnalysisResultCount +
   165	                experiment.failedAnalysisResultCount !=
   166	            experiment.analysisResultCount ||
   167	        experiment.completedAnalysisResultCount > 1 ||
   168	        experiment.failedAnalysisResultCount > 1 ||
   169	        (experiment.completedAnalysisResultCount > 0 &&
   170	         experiment.failedAnalysisResultCount > 0))
   171	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_analysis_evidence_invalid");
   172	
   173	    if (member.consistency == RecommendationCampaignStatusConsistency::inconsistent)
   174	        return member;
   175	
   176	    if (!input.activationId)
   177	    {
   178	        if (experiment.status == "paused" && experiment.phase == "train" &&
   179	            !experiment.workerPid && !experiment.currentOperation &&
   180	            !experiment.currentEpoch && !experiment.workerStartedAt &&
   181	            !experiment.startedAt && !experiment.completedAt &&
   182	            !experiment.exitCode && !experiment.errorMessage && !experiment.modelId)
   183	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::paused;
   184	        else
   185	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_unactivated_lifecycle_invalid");
   186	        return member;
   187	    }
   188	
   189	    if (experiment.status == "pending")
   190	    {
   191	        if (experiment.workerPid)
   192	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_pending_worker_invalid");
   193	        else if (experiment.phase == "train" || experiment.phase == "infer" ||
   194	                 experiment.phase == "analyze")
   195	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::pending;
   196	        else
   197	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_pending_phase_invalid");
   198	    }
   199	    else if (experiment.status == "running")
   200	    {
   201	        if (!experiment.workerStartedAt || !experiment.currentOperation ||
   202	            *experiment.currentOperation != experiment.phase)
   203	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_running_worker_evidence_invalid");
   204	        else if (experiment.phase == "train")
   205	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::runningTrain;
   206	        else if (experiment.phase == "infer")
   207	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::runningInfer;
   208	        else if (experiment.phase == "analyze")
   209	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::runningAnalyze;
   210	        else
   211	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_running_phase_invalid");
   212	    }
   213	    else if (experiment.status == "paused")
   214	    {
   215	        if (experiment.startedAt || experiment.currentEpoch || experiment.modelId)
   216	            member.operationalState =
   217	                RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::pausedAfterProgress;
   218	        else
   219	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_activated_experiment_still_paused");
   220	    }
   221	    else if (experiment.status == "completed")
   222	    {
   223	        if (experiment.phase != "done" || !experiment.completedAt ||
   224	            experiment.workerPid || !member.trainComplete ||
   225	            !member.inferConfigured || !member.inferComplete ||
   226	            !member.analyzeConfigured || !member.analyzeComplete ||
   227	            (experiment.errorMessage && !experiment.errorMessage->empty()) ||
   228	            (experiment.exitCode && *experiment.exitCode != 0))
   229	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_completed_evidence_invalid");
   230	        else
   231	        {
   232	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::completed;
   233	            member.terminalResult = RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::succeeded;
   234	        }
   235	    }
   236	    else if (experiment.status == "failed")
   237	    {
   238	        if (!experiment.completedAt || !experiment.errorMessage ||
   239	            experiment.errorMessage->empty() || experiment.workerPid)
   240	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_failed_evidence_invalid");
   241	        else
   242	        {
   243	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::failed;
   244	            member.terminalResult = RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::failed;
   245	        }
   246	    }
   247	    else if (experiment.status == "cancelled")
   248	    {
   249	        if (!experiment.completedAt || experiment.workerPid)
   250	            Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_cancelled_evidence_invalid");
   251	        else
   252	        {
   253	            member.operationalState = RecommendationCampaignStatusOperationalState::cancelled;
   254	            member.terminalResult = RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::cancelled;
   255	        }
   256	    }
   257	    else if (experiment.status != "pending" && experiment.status != "running" &&
   258	             experiment.status != "paused")
   259	        Diagnose(member, "campaign_status_experiment_lifecycle_invalid");
   260	
   261	    return member;
   262	}

   264	std::string OptionalLongLong(const std::optional<long long>& value)
   265	{
   266	    return value ? std::to_string(*value) : "null";
   267	}

   270	{

  ✓ Evaluate metric extensibility, lifecycle/evidence distinctions, invariants, ordering, and scope
  ✓ Run read-only verification and deliver severity-ranked architectural recommendation
codex
## Executive summary

Step 5a is **not ready to commit**. The implementation is pure, immutable in normal use, deterministic, and within scope, but its public contract has two blocking architectural defects:

- It supports only one metric per member.
- It collapses lifecycle and evidence states that Step 5b must distinguish.

Fixing these after Step 5b begins would require changing repository mappings, canonical identity, summaries, tests, and likely the version-1 contract.

Repository verification:

- Branch `phase5` is current with `github/phase5`.
- HEAD: `8b9939e1f8862e49d7411453baecd43fbe5a7c82`.
- Fast-forward-only pull reported “Already up to date.”
- Focused unit test passed with warnings treated as errors.
- Xcode Debug build succeeded.
- `git diff --check` and cached check passed.
- No files were modified during review.

## Findings

### Critical — Only one metric is representable

Both source and result evidence contain a singular `metric`, and each member contains one singular comparison: [OutcomeAssessment.hpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp:120>).

The summary also enforces exactly one comparison classification and at most one delta per member: [OutcomeAssessment.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.cpp:443>).

Consequences:

- A member cannot simultaneously report inference accuracy and leader score.
- Adding a third metric would require redesigning evidence, comparison, summary, canonical identity, and repository mapping.
- Duplicating a campaign member once per metric would break member-count and identity semantics.

This fails question 1.

### Critical — Terminal outcomes are collapsed

`RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState` contains only `NotTerminal` and `Terminal`: [OutcomeAssessment.hpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp:139>).

After excluding `NotTerminal`, every other result is treated as scientifically comparable: [OutcomeAssessment.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.cpp:551>).

Therefore:

- Successful completion is not explicitly represented.
- Failed and cancelled experiments cannot be represented distinctly.
- Mapping either to `Terminal` could incorrectly produce `Comparable`.
- Mapping them to absent result evidence produces `MissingResultEvidence`.
- Inconsistent evidence has no state or diagnostic representation.

This also regresses the already-established Step 4 contract, which distinguishes `succeeded`, `failed`, `cancelled`, `unknown`, and consistency independently: [CampaignStatus.hpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignStatus.hpp:49>).

### Critical — Nonterminal and malformed states cannot always be represented

`ResultEvidence` requires a positive experiment ID, context, and metric even for `NotTerminal`: [OutcomeAssessment.hpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp:145>).

A proposal-only member has no experiment ID, so Step 5b would have to omit `resultEvidence`, classifying it as `MissingResultEvidence` instead of nonterminal. Similar ambiguity arises for missing or malformed execution/experiment chains.

Downstream malformed evidence should remain visible as an `Inconsistent` member with deterministic diagnostics. Only untrustworthy top-level materialization identity/membership should fail the whole build.

### High — Result provenance is not validated

The builder validates source-experiment provenance, but not whether `resultEvidence.experimentId` is the exact experiment belonging to that materialized member, nor whether the same result experiment is reused across members: [OutcomeAssessment.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.cpp:649>).

The resulting identity is deterministic for the supplied evidence, but does not establish that the evidence belongs to the member.

### Medium — Immutable outputs are not valid-by-construction

The builder creates coherent output, but public member and aggregate constructors allow callers to assemble contradictory comparisons, summaries, members, and identities. The aggregate constructor validates only `observedAt`: [OutcomeAssessment.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.cpp:519>).

Output construction should be factory-controlled or perform complete cross-field validation.

### Positive — Step 5a boundary is respected

The implementation remains within the intended boundary:

- Pure domain types and derivation
- Immutable output fields
- Finite numeric and positive-ID validation
- Contiguous ordinal and uniqueness checks
- Deterministic member ordering
- Fixed context-difference ordering
- Locale-independent canonicalization and hashing
- `observedAt` correctly excluded from evidence identity
- No repository, PostgreSQL, persistence, service, CLI, scheduler, worker, or filesystem logic

## Risks

Beginning Step 5b with this contract would:

- Bake lossy lifecycle mappings into the repository.
- Misreport failed or cancelled experiments as comparable, missing, or generic terminal.
- Count proposal-only members as missing-result evidence.
- Force a version-1 identity redesign when multiple metrics are added.
- Permit unrelated or duplicated experiment evidence to enter a valid assessment.
- Produce summaries that cannot express completed-with-failures, cancellation, mixed terminal outcomes, or inconsistency.

## Recommended changes

Before Step 5b:

1. Separate lifecycle, consistency, and scientific comparison.

   - Terminal result: `NotTerminal`, `Succeeded`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`, `Unknown`.
   - Consistency: `Consistent`, `Inconsistent`, with deterministic diagnostic codes.
   - Keep `MissingResultEvidence` as a scientific evidence condition, not a substitute for lifecycle state.
   - Derive explicit member outcomes such as `NotReady`, `SucceededComparable`, `SucceededContextChanged`, `SucceededMetricGap`, `TerminalFailed`, `TerminalCancelled`, and `Inconsistent`.

2. Replace singular metrics with deterministic metric collections.

   - Source and result evidence should contain metric vectors.
   - Reject duplicate metric keys.
   - Canonically order metrics and comparisons by metric identity.
   - Produce one comparison per metric.
   - Keep per-member outcome counts separate from per-metric comparison/delta counts.

3. Make successful-result payload optional by lifecycle.

   Failed, cancelled, inconsistent, and proposal-only members must not require fabricated context, model, result IDs, or metric values.

4. Add exact result provenance.

   Include the expected execution/experiment identity or validated Step 4 member evidence, enforce result-to-member alignment, and reject duplicate experiment/result IDs across members.

5. Protect output invariants.

   Restrict derived output constructors to the builder or validate the full aggregate, including identity/content agreement.

6. Add tests for:

   - Inference accuracy and leader score on the same member
   - A future third metric and shuffled metric order
   - Duplicate metric identities
   - Successful, failed, cancelled, nonterminal, and inconsistent members
   - Proposal-only members not becoming `MissingResultEvidence`
   - Malformed downstream evidence retained with diagnostics
   - Cross-member result reuse/provenance mismatch
   - Aggregate precedence and identity changes for every state

## Final recommendation

**Do not commit yet.**

The implementation is mechanically sound and correctly scoped, but the metric cardinality and lifecycle/evidence contracts need correction before Step 5b begins.

### Critical — Terminal outcomes are collapsed

`RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultState` contains only `NotTerminal` and `Terminal`: [OutcomeAssessment.hpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp:139>).

After excluding `NotTerminal`, every other result is treated as scientifically comparable: [OutcomeAssessment.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.cpp:551>).

Therefore:

- Successful completion is not explicitly represented.
- Failed and cancelled experiments cannot be represented distinctly.
- Mapping either to `Terminal` could incorrectly produce `Comparable`.
- Mapping them to absent result evidence produces `MissingResultEvidence`.
- Inconsistent evidence has no state or diagnostic representation.

This also regresses the already-established Step 4 contract, which distinguishes `succeeded`, `failed`, `cancelled`, `unknown`, and consistency independently: [CampaignStatus.hpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignStatus.hpp:49>).

### Critical — Nonterminal and malformed states cannot always be represented

`ResultEvidence` requires a positive experiment ID, context, and metric even for `NotTerminal`: [OutcomeAssessment.hpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp:145>).

A proposal-only member has no experiment ID, so Step 5b would have to omit `resultEvidence`, classifying it as `MissingResultEvidence` instead of nonterminal. Similar ambiguity arises for missing or malformed execution/experiment chains.

Downstream malformed evidence should remain visible as an `Inconsistent` member with deterministic diagnostics. Only untrustworthy top-level materialization identity/membership should fail the whole build.

### High — Result provenance is not validated

The builder validates source-experiment provenance, but not whether `resultEvidence.experimentId` is the exact experiment belonging to that materialized member, nor whether the same result experiment is reused across members: [OutcomeAssessment.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.cpp:649>).

The resulting identity is deterministic for the supplied evidence, but does not establish that the evidence belongs to the member.

### Medium — Immutable outputs are not valid-by-construction

The builder creates coherent output, but public member and aggregate constructors allow callers to assemble contradictory comparisons, summaries, members, and identities. The aggregate constructor validates only `observedAt`: [OutcomeAssessment.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.cpp:519>).

Output construction should be factory-controlled or perform complete cross-field validation.

### Positive — Step 5a boundary is respected

The implementation remains within the intended boundary:

- Pure domain types and derivation
- Immutable output fields
- Finite numeric and positive-ID validation
- Contiguous ordinal and uniqueness checks
- Deterministic member ordering
- Fixed context-difference ordering
- Locale-independent canonicalization and hashing
- `observedAt` correctly excluded from evidence identity
- No repository, PostgreSQL, persistence, service, CLI, scheduler, worker, or filesystem logic

## Risks

Beginning Step 5b with this contract would:

- Bake lossy lifecycle mappings into the repository.
- Misreport failed or cancelled experiments as comparable, missing, or generic terminal.
- Count proposal-only members as missing-result evidence.
- Force a version-1 identity redesign when multiple metrics are added.
- Permit unrelated or duplicated experiment evidence to enter a valid assessment.
- Produce summaries that cannot express completed-with-failures, cancellation, mixed terminal outcomes, or inconsistency.

## Recommended changes

Before Step 5b:

1. Separate lifecycle, consistency, and scientific comparison.

   - Terminal result: `NotTerminal`, `Succeeded`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`, `Unknown`.
   - Consistency: `Consistent`, `Inconsistent`, with deterministic diagnostic codes.
   - Keep `MissingResultEvidence` as a scientific evidence condition, not a substitute for lifecycle state.
   - Derive explicit member outcomes such as `NotReady`, `SucceededComparable`, `SucceededContextChanged`, `SucceededMetricGap`, `TerminalFailed`, `TerminalCancelled`, and `Inconsistent`.

2. Replace singular metrics with deterministic metric collections.

   - Source and result evidence should contain metric vectors.
   - Reject duplicate metric keys.
   - Canonically order metrics and comparisons by metric identity.
   - Produce one comparison per metric.
   - Keep per-member outcome counts separate from per-metric comparison/delta counts.

3. Make successful-result payload optional by lifecycle.

   Failed, cancelled, inconsistent, and proposal-only members must not require fabricated context, model, result IDs, or metric values.

4. Add exact result provenance.

   Include the expected execution/experiment identity or validated Step 4 member evidence, enforce result-to-member alignment, and reject duplicate experiment/result IDs across members.

5. Protect output invariants.

   Restrict derived output constructors to the builder or validate the full aggregate, including identity/content agreement.

6. Add tests for:

   - Inference accuracy and leader score on the same member
   - A future third metric and shuffled metric order
   - Duplicate metric identities
   - Successful, failed, cancelled, nonterminal, and inconsistent members
   - Proposal-only members not becoming `MissingResultEvidence`
   - Malformed downstream evidence retained with diagnostics
   - Cross-member result reuse/provenance mismatch
   - Aggregate precedence and identity changes for every state

## Final recommendation

**Do not commit yet.**

The implementation is mechanically sound and correctly scoped, but the metric cardinality and lifecycle/evidence contracts need correction before Step 5b begins.
